# 绝地潜兵2 — 战争债券

> 本文件汇总 26 条战争债券资料；实体名称为二级标题，可直接按名称和别名检索。
> 数据源为社区整理，数值可能随游戏版本变化。

## 名称及别名索引

- **Borderline Justice** · ID: `borderline-justice`
- **Castellan's Creed** · ID: `castellans-creed` · 别名：Castellan’s Creed
- **Chemical Agents** · ID: `chemical-agents`
- **Control Group** · ID: `control-group`
- **Cutting Edge** · ID: `cutting-edge`
- **Democratic Detonation** · ID: `democratic-detonation`

- **Exo Experts** · ID: `exo-experts`
- **Force of Law** · ID: `force-of-law`
- **Freedom's Flame** · ID: `freedoms-flame`
- **Helldivers Mobilize!** · ID: `helldivers-mobilize` · 别名：Helldivers Mobilize
- **Ironclad Democracy** · ID: `ironclad-democracy` · 别名：Ironclad DemocracyP2、Ironclad DemocracyP3
- **Masters of Ceremony** · ID: `masters-of-ceremony`
- **Obedient Democracy Support Troopers** · ID: `obedient-democracy-support-troopers` · 别名：Halo: ODST
- **Polar Patriots** · ID: `polar-patriots`
- **Python Commandos** · ID: `python-commandos`
- **Redacted Regiment** · ID: `redacted-regiment`
- **Righteous Revenants** · ID: `righteous-revenants`
- **Scope of Authority** · ID: `scope-of-authority` · 别名：Scope of AuthorityP1、Scope of AuthorityP2、Scope of AuthorityP3
- **Servants of Freedom** · ID: `servants-of-freedom`
- **Siege Breakers** · ID: `siege-breakers`
- **Steeled Veterans** · ID: `steeled-veterans`
- **Truth Enforcers** · ID: `truth-enforcers`
- **Urban Legends** · ID: `urban-legends`

## Viper Commandos

**分类：** 战争债券 (`warbonds`) · **ID：** `viper-commandos`

**Wiki 原始词条：** https://helldivers.wiki.gg/wiki/Viper_Commandos_Premium_Warbond

### 简介

Viper Commandos is a Premium Warbond available for 1,000 Super Credits in the Warbonds tab of the Acquisition Center.
